STATE BANK OF INDIA 

Bombay, the 29th March 1973 

The following appointment on the Bank’s staff is 
hereby notified : — 

Shri D. I. A, D 'Souza has been appointed as Branch 
Inspector on the Central Office staff as from 29th March 
1973 . | 3 ! 

The 2nd April 1973 

The following appointment on the Bank's staff is 
hereby notified : — 

Shri P. N. Saxena has been appointed as Deputy 
Branch Inspector on the Central Office staff as from 2nd 
April 1973, 

T. R. VARADACHARY 
M ana %ing Director 


Bombay the 29th March 1973 

SBS. No. 1/1973. — It is hereby notified for general 
information that Shri Kesho Ram Passey, who was nomi- 
nated as a Director on the Board of the State Bank of 
Patiala under proviso to clause (d) of sub-section (1) 
of Section 25 of the State Bank of India (Subsidiary 
Banks) Act, 1959, ceased to be a Director with effect 
from the 26th March 1973 as he has resigned his 
Directorship voluntarily. 

Sd./- ILLEGIBLE 
Chairman 


STATE BANK OF INDORE 
Head Office 

Indore, the 9th April 1973 

No. 1/1973. — In partial modification of our notifica- 
tion No. 1/1972 dated the 11th February, 1972, publish- 
ed iu the Gazette of India, Part III (Section 4) dated 
the 1st April 1972, it is notified that in pursuance of 
Regulation 55 (i) of Subsidiary Banks General Regula- 
tions, 1959, the Board of Directors of the State Bank 
of Indore, has empowered the employees mentioned in 
column (1) hereunder to exercise the powrs specified 
in column No. (2)— 

Special Assistants — To pass independently cash, clear- 
ing and transfer cheques, vouchers, (whether credits or 
debits) Government and others, upto and including 
Rs. 5,000/-. 

(ii) To draw cheques, drafts, pay orders. 

(iii) To discharge, endorse cheques, bills, drafts and 
other negotiable instruments, 

M. V. BHIDE 
General Manager 


THE INSTITUTE OF CHARTERED ACCOUNTANTS 
OF INDIA 

New Delhi-X, the 28 th March 1973 

No. 8-CA(l)/17/72-73.— In pursuance of Clause (iii) 
of Regulation 10(1) of the Chartered Accountants Regu- 
lations 1964, it is hereby notified that the Certificates 
of Practice issued to Shri Harikant Mehta of Dr. Dave 
Building, Opposite Jharia Gujarati School, Top Floor, 
P.O. Jharia, (Dbanbad), shall stand cancelled from 1st 
March, 1973 to 30th June, 1973 as he does not desire 
to hold his Certificate of Practice (M. No. 13404). 

The 31sf March 1973 

No. A-CA (1) /31/72-73. — Jn pursuance of Regulation 
16 of the Chartered Accountants Regulations, 1964, it is 
hereby notified that in exercise of the powers concerred 
by clause (a) of Sub-sectjon (1) of Section 20 of the 
Chartered Accountants Act, 1949, the Council of the 
Institute of Chartered Accountants of India, has removed

New Delhi- 1, the 6th April 1973 

No. 1 1-1 ,'73-INC. The following declaration made by a resolution passed at a meeting of the Indian Nurs- 
ing Council held on 27th September, 1972, under Section 10 of the Indian Nursing Council Act, 1947 (48 of 
19 7), is hereby published, as required by Sub-Section (I) of Section 15 of the said Act, namely : — 


To recognise the Certificate of Auxiliary Nurse- 
Midwifery issued by the Board of Examiners in Auxi- 
liary Nurse-Midwifery set up by the Andaman Nicobar 
Islands Administration. 


WHEREAS the Board of Examiners in Auxiliary 
Nurse-Midwifery Course, Andaman Medical Department, 
set up by the Andaman Nicobar Islands Administration 
being an authority recognised by the Chief Commissio- 
ner, Andaman and Nicobar Islands Administration Union 
Territory for the purpose of granting qualification in 
Auxiliary Nurse-Midwife has applied to Indian Nursing 
Council constituted under the Indian Nursing Council 
Act, 1947 (XLVIII of 1947) that the Certificate granted 
by the Board of Examiners in Auxiliary Nurse-Midwifery 
Course, Andaman Medical Deportment, set up by the 
Andaman Nicobar Islands Administration be recognised 
for the purpose of the said Act; 

NOW, the Council, at its meeting held on 27th Sep- 
tember, 1972 for the said purpose, resolved that the 
aforesaid qualification when granted on or after 16th 
December, 1963 shall be recognised qualification for 
the purpose of the said Act, namely : — 

‘‘Auxiliary Nurse-Midwifery Certificate granted by the 
Board of Examiners in Auxiliary Nutse-Mldwifery set 
up by the Andaman Nicobar Islands Administration on 
or after 16th December, 1963. 

MISS A. CHERIAN 
Secretary
